- **CEO-291 (2026-09-05 11:5x CDT; `date` read 11:52:04 at the MODE rewrite) — LON'S THREE ORDERS IN ONE SITTING: THE CEO TAKES THE ICON SERIAL ROW · THE GLOBAL IS GRANTED · MODE → QUARTET · A FIFTH HQ ROOT.** (1) Lon, verbatim: *"Take the Icon serial row yourself."* — `icon-program-visible-list-serial-burned-by-driver-args-staging-at-startup` CLAIMED:ceo (hq_C told it leaves its lane). MECHANISM, oracle-settled: iconx builds the argument list before `main` ONLY when `main` takes a parameter (`main()` → `list_1(3)` with or without args; `main(args)` → `list_2(3)` and args is `list_1`); SCRIP built it unconditionally since `1d31e2963` removed the nparams gates so HOST could read its arguments — but HOST never needs the list (selector 2 reads the raw command line, selector 3 needs only the count). THE CURE: staging split from binding — `rt_main_args_stage(v,n)` stores the raw vector and count and draws no serial; new `rt_main_args_bind()` materialises the list into `g_call_args[0]` and is called (m3) or emitted (m4) at the four driver sites only when the entry graph's `nparams >= 1`; `rt_main_args_fetch()` binds lazily; `rt_main_args_count()` returns the raw count. ⛔ GLOBAL GRANT, Lon in-chat verbatim: *"Grant the global request."* — for `static char **g_main_args_v` in `src/runtime/by_name_dispatch.c` (holds the raw argv from the startup prologue until an arbitrary later HOST() call or the bind; nothing on the stack or in a register survives to it); `g_main_args_staged` repurposed as `g_main_args_n` (−1 = unstaged), net one new file-static. So: ONE library."* ⭐ **ONE LIBRARY IS LAW** — runtime compilation is a universal cross-language feature (Icon+Snocone to gain EVAL/CODE; Prolog's assertz already is it; Pascal/Raku probably not), so every compiled program keeps the compiler reachable by design. hq_P's steps 0–5 proceed unchanged; the registration-vtable grant in (1) stands but is likely unneeded with the split dead. **AND THE SHRINK VISION, Lon same breath (routed as direction, not a row):** the library gets much smaller later by (a) replacing ALL C parsers with the self-hosted Snocone parsers (`SCRIP/bootstrap/`, the 6-parser frontend born at CEO-9) linked into SCRIP, and (b) dynamically loading/unloading templates via `*(EXPRESSION)` emitting BOTH binary and text code — *"a string value in the language will hold asm code that can run direct on the CPU."* **CEO-11c — THE EMPIRICAL SWITCH RULE (Lon, same sitting, verbatim in substance):** *"Too many switches for me to weed through. ## ⭐⭐⭐ WAVE-3 RUN PLAN (CEO proposal to Lon, s257 — executes only on Lon's go)

**Verdict: retry at 8 seats on a repaired control plane, with mechanical abort criteria.** Basis: scorecard FINDING (fleet 1.5–3.2x/day over watched week, retractions flat); retrospective (all wave-2 killers mechanical); law 5 (fleet size follows queue readiness — 16 was never earned).

**PHASE 0 — PRE-FLIGHT: ⭐ EXECUTED BY hq_C AND hq_P THEMSELVES (Lon s257, in-chat: "the PRE-FLIGHT could be done by claude_C and claude_P") — ~half a day, ALL gated by negative tests.** This is inside the HQ lane: the s256 delegate-only rule (superseded s259) binds on COMPILER defects; the control plane is the HQs' own instrument, and routing its repair through the broken picker would be the seat13 race re-enacted. Division so the twins never collide in one file: **hq_C takes V2-1 (picker+assign) and the queue purge (item 2); hq_P takes V2-3 (banner) and V2-4 (identity/mailboxes/ceo remap); task-file conversion (item 5) each HQ converts its own 8.** ⛔ CROSS-VERIFICATION MANDATORY: each HQ runs the negative tests on the OTHER's rungs before the firing gate counts them green — LAW 0 applied to the repair crew. V2-5 (gate honesty sweep, 31 scripts) stays FLEET work — it is not firing-gate-blocking because the first 16 task files prove their own DONE-WHENs can say NO individually.
1. V2-1 picker: rank-sorted free rows + `s4e_msg.sh assign <seat> <topic>` + assigned-first `next` (kills the dispatch race and the rank-blind burying).
2. Queue purge: 112 DONE rows → `QUEUE.done.tsv`; blank line at :145 deleted; seat13/seat14 dead locks released; dup rows (`diag-reg-*`) pruned; every surviving brief pointer verified.
3. V2-3 banner: HQ ✅-refusal while inbox mail >30 min unread; board line gains oldest-unanswered age + row topic.
4. V2-4 identity assert + census-by-mailbox (unblinds hq_C/hq_P); deliver the marooned messages (claude01 ×2, `.msg.b1QC4J`); retire the phantom mailbox; **remap `/home/claude` → identity `ceo` (Lon s257) and retire the legacy `hq` mailbox after the HQs drain its 29-message backlog.**
5. Convert ONLY the top ~16 dispatchable rows to `tasks/*.task.md` (8 per HQ), each DONE-WHEN demonstrated able to say NO. Not all 55 — no ocean-boiling.
**Firing gate (AMENDED CEO s264 — the Q=0 item is VOID, hq_C's coma finding: queue depth measures the PRODUCER, never the CONSUMER):** a ROUND TRIP per seat — a message sent to each running seat is demonstrably read and answered within that session (receipt, not depth; RULES.md § THE MAIL CHANNEL: PRESENCE IS NOT PROOF); every seat root to be used passes the oracle preflight (absent-oracle false-FAIL class); both HQ cursors pushed.

**PHASE 1 — THE RUN: ⛔ AMENDED BY LON s257 mid-proposal, in-chat: "let's make that /home/claude [CEO]. 2 HQ's. And 16 FLEET members." — 16 seats (8 per HQ), superseding CEO's 8-first recommendation (recorded, not re-argued).** The mitigation that makes 16 safe is the FIRING GATE: no seat fires until preflight is green AND a ready task file exists for it (16 task files, 8 per HQ, SNOBOL4-first per both charters). Dispatch by `assign` only (no self-pick day 1). HQs run drain→verify→mint→assign strictly. CEO audits twice (sampled DONE-WHEN re-runs) from its ruled root `/home/claude`. Lon reads computed banners/board only, fires /clear, spot-audits at will. At 16 seats the abort criteria below are not decoration — they are what shrinks the fleet mechanically before starvation replays wave 2.

**TARGETS, measured exactly as the scorecard FINDING (defined BEFORE the run, per LAW 0):** commit→row traceability >70% (was 34%) · median question-wait <30 min (was 45m–1h51m) · rework share of FINDINGs <15% (wave-2 day: 17%) · corpus watermark net-positive on stable denominator · zero re-dispatch of landed work · retractions ≤ wave-1 rate.

**ABORT/SHRINK, mechanical:** >6 unanswered questions for 30 min → HQs stop minting and drain (banner enforces) · 2 dispatch mismatches → halt, fix picker, resume · any seat 2h with no ledger delta → its task re-FREEs via β and reassigns (the baton working as designed).

**SCALE RULE (amended by Lon s257 — 16 is the ruled size):** the surviving half of the rule: never grow or restructure mid-day, and never simultaneously with a namespace change or an HQ reorganization (wave 2's compounding error). If the abort criteria shrink the fleet, re-growing to 16 waits for the next day's green preflight. ⏱ Note: the Claude Code 50% weekly-limit promotion currently ends Aug 31 — the cheap window for wave 3 is the next ~9 days.

## THE CEO LOOP (one loop, per ARCH-FLEET-CEO.md; everything else is out of lane)

⭐ **THE AUDIT READS A STANDALONE rc (ceo 2026-09-06 11:5x, CEO-328 (7), hq_U's words via hq_T and hq_R):** in a fail-on-first-red set every arm below the first red reports nothing, and nothing is indistinguishable from unchanged — a green quoted from a truncated `make test` is unearned; the ceo audits a gate's state from the gate run on its own, never from a `make test` log, and asks a seat quoting a later arm whether it ran that arm standalone.

⛔⭐⭐⭐ **FLIPS FIRST (Lon 2026-09-06 09:54 CDT, in-chat to ceo, verbatim: *"Just so we are clear. I will never be running that shell script. However you will."*; CEO-320).** Every ceo tick and every health check BEGINS with the flip histogram from the progress database — `python3 .github/scripts/util_progress_flips.py --since 24h --per hour --names` — reported to Lon as a table: per hour, programs that went from failing to passing and back, OUR TEST-SUITE PROGRAMS versus THIRD-PARTY PACKAGE PROGRAMS, by name when asked. The ceo runs it; Lon is never handed a command to run. A tick whose report does not lead with it is a defective tick. Zero package flips in a report is stated as ZERO, never softened. The database is `/home/resources/progress/results.tsv` (README beside it); until hq_T's append row lands, the ceo's replay rows (`ceo-replay`) are the only rows, and the report says so.

⭐ **AUDIT AMENDMENT (ceo 2026-09-03 21:12 CDT, on hq_B's ICN4 false green):** an audit that only re-runs a row's DONE-WHEN inherits that DONE-WHEN's blind spot — ICN4's criterion graded against `ALL.ref`, the ref was wrong, the cure matched the wrong ref, and the ceo's re-run returned GREEN faithfully. From here a ceo audit of a CORRECTNESS row re-grades at least one sampled entry against the ORACLE (`icont`/`iconx`, `sbl -bf`, `swipl`/`gprolog`, `fpc -Miso`, Rakudo), never only against the ref, and says so in the audit line; a ref is evidence about a past oracle run, not about the oracle (law candidate (d)). ⭐ ADDENDUM (21:21, hq_B's cross-suite gate fixture bug): an audit RE-DERIVES THE POPULATION, not only the verdict — hq_B's gate once printed a confident green over 10 pairs where 76 existed because an unordered `listdir` picked `ALL.trace` as the master; the ceo's audit line names the population it saw (e.g. "70 pairs of 76 origin-linked entries") beside rc.

1. **AUDIT** — re-run the DONE-WHEN of ~3 sampled DONE tasks; a red sample is a reopened task + a one-line ruling to the owning HQ, never a CEO fix.
2. **ARBITRATE** — rule every `ceo/inbox` escalation; the ruling routes to the task file + the owning GOAL/ARCH file, and the reply cites where.
3. **LAW** — land at most ONE law change per session (HQs propose; CEO lands into RULES/ARCH/PROTOCOL-draft with its receipt).
4. **STRATEGY** — with Lon only. CEO does not talk to seats, write briefs, or measure anything.
5. **WALK** (Lon 2026-09-01: *"Make a master plan and being CEO ensure the fleet walks along the ladders."*) — run `util_ladder_walk.py` against `MASTER-PLAN.md`; re-rank so rungs sit at 0/1 and off-ladder work at ≥2; telegram each HQ the rungs it owes (TO-MINT) and the orphans it must place; rewrite the plan's tables in place from the output; aim the AUDIT step at DONE rungs. The plan is the order of the work; the walk is the instrument; the ceo never works a rung.

⛔ CEO context is the most expensive context in the fleet: computed digests only — `fleet`, the two HQ cursors, escalations, and the specific task files under audit. Reading a raw log or a goal-file body is the CEO equivalent of HQ curing.
